Implement the following plan:

# Handle duplicate code Z-Wave notification (Issue #848)

## Context

When a lock rejects a code because it duplicates a code in another slot, Z-Wave sends an Access Control notification (type 6, event 15: `NEW_USER_CODE_NOT_ADDED_DUE_TO_DUPLICATE_CODE`). LCM currently ignores this notification, so the in-sync sensor sees the code isn't set, retries, gets rejected again, and loops indefinitely.

**Goal**: When this notification is received, disable the affected slot, log an error, and create a persistent notification so the user knows to set a unique code.

## Changes

### 1. `providers/zwave_js.py` — Handle duplicate code notification

**Add `_set_in_progress_code_slot: int | None` field** to `ZWaveJSLock` (default `None`). Lifecycle:
- **Set** in `async_set_usercode` before the service call
- **Cleared on success** in `on_value_updated` (inside `subscribe_push_updates`) when a real value update arrives for the in-progress slot — this confirms the lock accepted the code
- **Cleared on failure** in `_handle_duplicate_code` when the notification is handled

**In `_handle_zwave_js_event`**, before the normal event flow, check for event 15 — but **only when `_set_in_progress_code_slot` is set**. This guards against reacting to duplicate code notifications triggered externally (e.g., someone manually adding a duplicate code at the lock's keypad). We only want to react when LCM initiated the set operation:

```python
if (
    evt.data[ATTR_EVENT] == AccessControlNotificationEvent.NEW_USER_CODE_NOT_ADDED_DUE_TO_DUPLICATE_CODE
    and self._set_in_progress_code_slot is not None
    # If userId is in params, verify it matches our in-progress slot
    # (otherwise the notification is about a different slot, not ours)
    and (code_slot == 0 or code_slot == self._set_in_progress_code_slot)
):
    self._handle_duplicate_code()
    return
```

**In `on_value_updated`** (inside `subscribe_push_updates`), clear the field when a real push update arrives for the in-progress slot (confirms lock accepted the code):

```python
if code_slot == self._set_in_progress_code_slot:
    self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
```

(Add this early in the handler, before the dedup/skip checks)

**Add `_handle_duplicate_code()` callback method** to `ZWaveJSLock` (no params — uses `_set_in_progress_code_slot` directly since we only enter this when it's set):
- Read `code_slot = self._set_in_progress_code_slot`
- Clear `_set_in_progress_code_slot`
- Find all LCM config entries managing this lock + slot (same pattern as `async_fire_code_slot_event`)
- For each matching config entry:
  - Disable the slot: `data[CONF_SLOTS][slot_num][CONF_ENABLED] = False` via `hass.config_entries.async_update_entry()`
  - Create persistent notification with ID `{DOMAIN}_{entry_id}_{slot}_duplicate_code`
- Log an error once (not per config entry)

**New imports**: `copy`, `from homeassistant.components.persistent_notification import async_create`, `from homeassistant.const import CONF_ENABLED`

### 2. `tests/providers/test_zwave_js.py` — Add tests

Tests following existing notification event test patterns:

1. **`test_duplicate_code_notification_disables_slot`** — Event 15 with `_set_in_progress_code_slot` set, matching userId → slot disabled in config entry, persistent notification created, error logged, field cleared
2. **`test_duplicate_code_notification_no_user_id_disables_slot`** — Event 15 with `_set_in_progress_code_slot` set, no userId in params → still handles it (uses in-progress slot)
3. **`test_duplicate_code_notification_ignored_when_not_in_progress`** — Event 15 arrives but `_set_in_progress_code_slot` is None (external trigger) → notification ignored, no slot disabled
4. **`test_duplicate_code_notification_ignored_when_user_id_mismatches`** — Event 15 with `_set_in_progress_code_slot` set to slot 2, but userId says slot 3 → ignored (not our operation)
5. **`test_set_in_progress_cleared_on_value_update`** — After `async_set_usercode`, a real push value update for that slot clears `_set_in_progress_code_slot`

## Files Modified

- `custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py` — notification handling + slot tracking
- `tests/providers/test_zwave_js.py` — new tests

    90→    """Class to represent ZWave JS lock."""
    91→

   104→    @functools.cached_property
   105→    def _usercode_cc_version(self) -> int:
   106→        """Return the User Code CC version supported by this node."""
   107→        version = next(
   108→            (
   109→                cc.version
   110→                for cc in self.node.command_classes
   111→                if cc.id == CommandClass.USER_CODE
   112→            ),
   113→            0,
   114→        )
   115→        if version == 0:
   116→            _LOGGER.warning(
   117→                "Lock %s: User Code CC not found on node %s. This may "
   118→                "indicate an incomplete interview. Defaulting to V1 behavior",
   119→                self.lock.entity_id,
   120→                self.node.node_id,
   121→            )
   122→            return 1
   123→        return version
   124→
   125→    @property
   126→    def supports_push(self) -> bool:
   127→        """
   128→        Return whether this lock supports push-based updates.
   129→
   130→        Z-Wave JS emits value update events when the cache changes, so we
   131→        subscribe to those instead of polling.
   132→        """
   133→        return True
   134→
   135→    @property
   136→    def supports_code_slot_events(self) -> bool:
   137→        """Return whether this lock supports code slot events."""
   138→        return True
   139→
   140→    @property
   141→    def connection_check_interval(self) -> timedelta | None:
   142→        """Z-Wave JS exposes config entry state changes, so skip polling."""
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    def _get_client_state(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   146→        """Return whether the Z-Wave JS client is ready and a retry reason."""
   147→        if self.lock_config_entry.state != ConfigEntryState.LOADED:
   148→            return False, "config entry not loaded"
   149→
   150→        runtime_data: ZwaveJSData | None = getattr(
   151→            self.lock_config_entry, "runtime_data", None
   152→        )
   153→        client: Client | None = (
   154→            getattr(runtime_data, "client", None) if runtime_data else None
   155→        )
   156→        if not client:
   157→            return False, "Z-Wave JS client not ready"
   158→
   159→        if not client.connected:
   160→            return False, "Z-Wave JS client not connected"
   161→
   162→        if client.driver is None:
   163→            return False, "Z-Wave JS driver not ready"
   164→
   165→        return True, ""
   166→
   167→    def code_slot_in_use(self, code_slot: int) -> bool | None:
   168→        """Return whether a code slot is in use."""
   169→        try:
   170→            return get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)[ATTR_IN_USE]
   171→        except (KeyError, ValueError):
   172→            return None
   173→
   174→    def _get_slot_entity_states(self, code_slot: int) -> tuple[str, str, str] | None:
   175→        """
   176→        Get active state, PIN value, and PIN entity ID for a managed slot.
   177→
   178→        Returns tuple of (active_state, pin_value, pin_entity_id) if the slot is
   179→        managed by LCM and entities exist, None otherwise.
   180→        """
   181→        try:
   182→            config_entry = next(
   183→                entry
   184→                for entry in self.hass.config_entries.async_entries(DOMAIN)
   185→                if self.lock.entity_id in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_LOCKS, [])
   186→                and code_slot in (int(s) for s in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_SLOTS, {}))
   187→            )
   188→        except StopIteration:
   189→            return None
   190→
   191→        base_unique_id = f"{config_entry.entry_id}|{code_slot}"
   192→        active_entity_id = self.ent_reg.async_get_entity_id(
   193→            BINARY_SENSOR_DOMAIN, DOMAIN, f"{base_unique_id}|{ATTR_ACTIVE}"
   194→        )
   195→        pin_entity_id = self.ent_reg.async_get_entity_id(
   196→            TEXT_DOMAIN, DOMAIN, f"{base_unique_id}|{CONF_PIN}"
   197→        )
   198→
   199→        if not active_entity_id or not pin_entity_id:
   200→            return None
   201→
   202→        active_state = self.hass.states.get(active_entity_id)
   203→        pin_state = self.hass.states.get(pin_entity_id)
   204→
   205→        if not active_state or not pin_state:
   206→            return None
   207→
   208→        return (active_state.state, pin_state.state, pin_entity_id)
   209→
   210→    def _slot_expects_pin(self, code_slot: int) -> bool:
   211→        """
   212→        Check if LCM expects a PIN on this slot (active=ON with PIN set).
   213→
   214→        Used to ignore stale userIdStatus=AVAILABLE events from locks that report
   215→        old status after a code was successfully set.
   216→        """
   217→        states = self._get_slot_entity_states(code_slot)
   218→        if states is None:
   219→            return False
   220→
   221→        active_state, pin_value, _ = states
   222→        return active_state == STATE_ON and bool(pin_value)
   223→
   224→    def _resolve_pin_if_masked(self, value: str, code_slot: int) -> str | None:
   225→        """
   226→        Resolve a PIN value, looking up expected PIN if masked.
   227→
   228→        Some locks return masked values (all asterisks) instead of the actual PIN.
   229→        This method returns the value as-is if not masked, or looks up the expected
   230→        PIN from LCM entities if masked.
   231→
   232→        """
   233→        slot_in_use = self.code_slot_in_use(code_slot)
   234→
   235→        if value == "0" * len(value) and slot_in_use is False:
   236→            # Some locks return all zeros instead of a blank value when cleared - treat
   237→            # as unmasked cleared value
   238→            return ""
   239→
   240→        # If not masked, return as-is
   241→        if not value or not (value == "*" * len(value) and slot_in_use):
   242→            return value
   243→
   244→        # Masked - look up expected PIN from LCM entities
   245→        # Use ATTR_ACTIVE binary sensor (not CONF_ENABLED switch) to match what
   246→        # sync logic expects. ATTR_ACTIVE considers conditions, so we only resolve
   247→        # masked codes when sync expects a PIN on the lock.
   248→        states = self._get_slot_entity_states(code_slot)
   249→        if states is None:
   250→            return None
   251→
   252→        active_state, pin_value, pin_entity_id = states
   253→
   254→        if active_state == STATE_ON:
   255→            _LOGGER.debug(
   256→                "PIN is masked for lock %s code slot %s, assuming value from PIN entity %s",
   257→                self.lock.entity_id,
   258→                code_slot,
   259→                pin_entity_id,
   260→            )
   261→            return pin_value
   262→
   263→        # Fall back to returning masked value if active state is not ON (e.g. slot not
   264→        # enabled) - we don't care what the value is, just that there is one so that
   265→        # the sync logic treats this slot as having a PIN set on the lock
   266→        return value
   267→
   268→    @callback
   269→    def subscribe_push_updates(self) -> None:
   270→        """Subscribe to User Code CC value update events."""
   271→        # Idempotent - skip if already subscribed
   272→        if self._value_update_unsub is not None:
   273→            return
   274→
   275→        ready, reason = self._get_client_state()
   276→        if not ready:
   277→            _LOGGER.debug(
   278→                "Lock %s: push subscription deferred (%s)",
   279→                self.lock.entity_id,
   280→                reason,
   281→            )
   282→            self._schedule_push_retry(reason)
   283→            return
   284→
   285→        @callback
   286→        def on_value_updated(event: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   287→            """Handle value update events from Z-Wave JS."""
   288→            args: dict[str, Any] = event["args"]
   289→            # Filter for User Code CC - handle both userCode and userIdStatus
   290→            if args.get("commandClass") != CommandClass.USER_CODE:
   291→                return
   292→
   293→            property_name = args.get("property")
   294→            if property_name not in (
   295→                LOCK_USERCODE_PROPERTY,
   296→                LOCK_USERCODE_STATUS_PROPERTY,
   297→            ):
   298→                return
   299→
   300→            code_slot = int(args["propertyKey"])
   301→
   302→            # Slot 0 is not a valid user code slot (used for status/metadata)
   303→            if code_slot == 0:
   304→                return
   305→
   306→            # Handle userIdStatus updates - slot cleared is a fast path
   307→            if property_name == LOCK_USERCODE_STATUS_PROPERTY:
   308→                status = args.get("newValue")
   309→                if status == CodeSlotStatus.AVAILABLE:
   310→                    # Ignore AVAILABLE status if LCM expects a PIN on this slot.
   311→                    # Some locks send stale AVAILABLE events after a code was set,
   312→                    # which would cause infinite sync loops.
   313→                    if self._slot_expects_pin(code_slot):
   314→                        _LOGGER.debug(
   315→                            "Lock %s: ignoring userIdStatus=AVAILABLE for slot %s "
   316→                            "(LCM expects PIN on this slot)",
   317→                            self.lock.entity_id,
   318→                            code_slot,
   319→                        )
   320→                        return
   321→
   322→                    # Slot was cleared - update coordinator if needed
   323→                    if self.coordinator and self.coordinator.data.get(code_slot) != "":
   324→                        _LOGGER.debug(
   325→                            "Lock %s: slot %s userIdStatus=AVAILABLE, marking cleared",
   326→                            self.lock.entity_id,
   327→                            code_slot,
   328→                        )
   329→                        self.coordinator.push_update({code_slot: ""})
   330→                return
   331→
   332→            # Handle userCode updates
   333→            # newValue is the raw PIN string (or None/empty/"0000" if cleared)
   334→            new_value = args.get("newValue")
   335→            # Treat empty, None, or all-zeros as cleared
   336→            if not new_value or (
   337→                isinstance(new_value, str) and new_value.strip("0") == ""
   338→            ):
   339→                value = ""
   340→            else:
   341→                value = str(new_value)
   342→
   343→            # Resolve masked codes (all asterisks) to expected PIN
   344→            # This prevents infinite sync loops when locks return masked values
   345→            resolved = self._resolve_pin_if_masked(value, code_slot)
   346→            if resolved is None:
   347→                _LOGGER.debug(
   348→                    "Lock %s: skipping masked code update for slot %s "
   349→                    "(unable to resolve)",
   350→                    self.lock.entity_id,
   351→                    code_slot,
   352→                )
   353→                return
   354→
   355→            # Skip if value hasn't changed (Z-Wave JS sends duplicate events)
   356→            if self.coordinator and self.coordinator.data.get(code_slot) == resolved:
   357→                return
   358→
   359→            _LOGGER.debug(
   360→                "Lock %s received push update for slot %s: %s",
   361→                self.lock.entity_id,
   362→                code_slot,
   363→                "****" if resolved else "(cleared)",
   364→            )
   365→
   366→            # Push update to coordinator
   367→            if self.coordinator:
   368→                self.coordinator.push_update({code_slot: resolved})
   369→
   370→        try:
   371→            self._value_update_unsub = self.node.on("value updated", on_value_updated)
   372→        except ValueError as err:
   373→            _LOGGER.debug(
   374→                "Lock %s push subscription deferred: %s", self.lock.entity_id, err
   375→            )
   376→            self._schedule_push_retry("node not ready")
   377→
   378→    def _schedule_push_retry(self, reason: str) -> None:
   379→        """Schedule a retry for push subscription if one isn't pending."""
   380→        if self._push_retry_cancel:
   381→            return
   382→
   383→        _LOGGER.debug(
   384→            "Lock %s: scheduling push subscription retry in %ss (%s)",
   385→            self.lock.entity_id,
   386→            PUSH_SUBSCRIBE_RETRY_DELAY,
   387→            reason,
   388→        )
   389→        self._push_retry_cancel = async_call_later(
   390→            self.hass, PUSH_SUBSCRIBE_RETRY_DELAY, self._handle_push_retry
   391→        )
   392→
   393→    @callback
   394→    def _handle_push_retry(self, _now: Any) -> None:
   395→        """Retry push subscription after delay."""
   396→        if self._push_retry_cancel:
   397→            self._push_retry_cancel()
   398→            self._push_retry_cancel = None
   399→        self.subscribe_push_updates()
   400→
   401→    @callback
   402→    def unsubscribe_push_updates(self) -> None:
   403→        """Unsubscribe from value update events."""
   404→        if self._push_retry_cancel:
   405→            self._push_retry_cancel()
   406→            self._push_retry_cancel = None
   407→        if self._value_update_unsub:
   408→            self._value_update_unsub()
   409→            self._value_update_unsub = None
   410→
   411→    @callback
   412→    def _zwave_js_event_filter(self, event_data: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   413→        """Filter out events."""
   414→        # Try to find the lock that we are getting an event for, skipping
   415→        # ones that don't match
   416→        assert self.node.client.driver
   417→        return (
   418→            event_data[ATTR_HOME_ID] == self.node.client.driver.controller.home_id
   419→            and event_data[ATTR_NODE_ID] == self.node.node_id
   420→            and event_data[ATTR_DEVICE_ID] == self.lock.device_id
   421→        )
   422→
   423→    @callback
   424→    def _handle_zwave_js_event(self, evt: Event) -> None:
   425→        """Handle Z-Wave JS event."""
   426→        if evt.data[ATTR_TYPE] != NotificationType.ACCESS_CONTROL:
   427→            _LOGGER.debug(
   428→                "Lock %s received non Access Control event: %s",
   429→                self.lock.entity_id,
   430→                evt.as_dict(),
   431→            )
   432→            return
   433→
   434→        params = evt.data.get(ATTR_PARAMETERS) or {}
   435→        code_slot = params.get("userId", 0)
   436→        self.async_fire_code_slot_event(
   437→            code_slot=code_slot,
   438→            to_locked=next(
   439→                (
   440→                    to_locked
   441→                    for to_locked, codes in ACCESS_CONTROL_NOTIFICATION_TO_LOCKED.items()
   442→                    if evt.data[ATTR_EVENT] in codes
   443→                ),
   444→                None,
   445→            ),
   446→            action_text=evt.data.get(ATTR_EVENT_LABEL),
   447→            source_data=evt,
   448→        )
   449→
   450→    @property
   451→    def domain(self) -> str:
   452→        """Return integration domain."""
   453→        return ZWAVE_JS_DOMAIN
   454→
   455→    async def async_setup(self, config_entry: ConfigEntry) -> None:
   456→        """Set up lock."""
   457→        await super().async_setup(config_entry)
   458→        self._listeners.append(
   459→            self.hass.bus.async_listen(
   460→                ZWAVE_JS_NOTIFICATION_EVENT,
   461→                self._handle_zwave_js_event,
   462→                self._zwave_js_event_filter,
   463→            )
   464→        )
   465→
   466→    async def async_unload(self, remove_permanently: bool) -> None:
   467→        """Unload lock."""
   468→        for listener in self._listeners:
   469→            listener()
   470→        self._listeners.clear()
   471→        await super().async_unload(remove_permanently)
   472→
   473→    async def async_is_connection_up(self) -> bool:
   474→        """Return whether connection to lock is up."""
   475→        ready, _reason = self._get_client_state()
   476→        return ready
   477→
   478→    async def async_hard_refresh_codes(self) -> dict[int, int | str]:
   479→        """
   480→        Perform hard refresh and return all codes.
   481→
   482→        Uses Z-Wave JS's refresh_cc_values which handles checksum optimization
   483→        internally - it will skip re-fetching codes if the checksum hasn't changed.
   484→        Returns codes in the same format as async_get_usercodes().
   485→        """
   486→        await self._async_refresh_usercode_cache()
   487→        return await self.async_get_usercodes()
   488→
   489→    async def async_set_usercode(
   490→        self, code_slot: int, usercode: int | str, name: str | None = None
   491→    ) -> bool:
   492→        """
   493→        Set a usercode on a code slot.
   494→
   495→        Returns True if the value was changed, False if already set to this value.
   496→        """
   497→        # Check if the code is already set to this value (avoid unnecessary network call)
   498→        usercode = str(usercode)
   499→        try:
   500→            if (current := get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)).get("in_use"):
   501→                current_code = str(current.get("usercode", ""))
   502→                # Resolve masked codes and compare
   503→                if usercode == self._resolve_pin_if_masked(current_code, code_slot):
   504→                    _LOGGER.debug(
   505→                        "Lock %s slot %s already has this PIN, skipping set",
   506→                        self.lock.entity_id,
   507→                        code_slot,
   508→                    )
   509→                    return False
   510→        except Exception:
   511→            # If we can't check the cache, proceed with the set
   512→            pass
   513→
   514→        service_data = {
   515→            ATTR_ENTITY_ID: self.lock.entity_id,
   516→            ATTR_CODE_SLOT: code_slot,
   517→            ATTR_USERCODE: usercode,
   518→        }
   519→        await self.async_call_service(
   520→            ZWAVE_JS_DOMAIN, SERVICE_SET_LOCK_USERCODE, service_data
   521→        )
   522→        # V1 locks don't reliably update the Z-Wave JS value cache after set.
   523→        # Poll the slot directly from the device to force-update the cache
   524→        # before the coordinator reads it, preventing sync loops.
   525→        # No try-except: if the poll fails, we must not proceed with the
   526→        # optimistic update since async_request_refresh() would overwrite it
   527→        # with stale cache data, reintroducing the sync loop. Letting the
   528→        # error propagate allows the sync mechanism to retry the operation.
   529→        if self._usercode_cc_version < 2:
   530→            await get_usercode_from_node(self.node, code_slot)
   531→        # Optimistic update: Z-Wave command succeeded (lock acknowledged), but the
   532→        # value cache updates asynchronously via push notification. Update coordinator
   533→        # immediately to prevent sync loops from reading stale cache data.
   534→        if self.coordinator:
   535→            self.coordinator.push_update({code_slot: usercode})
   536→        return True
   537→
   538→    async def async_clear_usercode(self, code_slot: int) -> bool:
   539→        """
   540→        Clear a usercode on a code slot.
   541→
   542→        Returns True if the value was changed, False if already cleared.
   543→        """
   544→        # Check if the slot is already cleared (avoid unnecessary network call)
   545→        try:
   546→            current = get_usercode(self.node, code_slot)
   547→            if not current.get("in_use"):
   548→                _LOGGER.debug(
   549→                    "Lock %s slot %s already cleared, skipping clear",
   550→                    self.lock.entity_id,
   551→                    code_slot,
   552→                )
   553→                return False
   554→        except Exception:
   555→            # If we can't check the cache, proceed with the clear
   556→            pass
   557→
   558→        service_data = {
   559→            ATTR_ENTITY_ID: self.lock.entity_id,
   560→            ATTR_CODE_SLOT: code_slot,
   561→        }
   562→        await self.async_call_service(
   563→            ZWAVE_JS_DOMAIN, SERVICE_CLEAR_LOCK_USERCODE, service_data
   564→        )
   565→        # V1 locks don't reliably update the Z-Wave JS value cache after clear.
   566→        # Poll the slot directly from the device to force-update the cache
   567→        # before the coordinator reads it, preventing sync loops.
   568→        # See comment in async_set_usercode for why this is not wrapped in
   569→        # try-except.
   570→        if self._usercode_cc_version < 2:
   571→            await get_usercode_from_node(self.node, code_slot)
   572→        # Optimistic update: Z-Wave command succeeded (lock acknowledged), but the
   573→        # value cache updates asynchronously via push notification. Update coordinator
   574→        # immediately to prevent sync loops from reading stale cache data.
   575→        if self.coordinator:
   576→            self.coordinator.push_update({code_slot: ""})
   577→        return True
   578→
   579→    def _get_usercodes_from_cache(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   580→        """Get usercodes from Z-Wave JS value DB cache."""
   581→        try:
   582→            return list(get_usercodes(self.node) or [])
   583→        except Exception as err:
   584→            raise LockDisconnected from err
   585→
   586→    async def _async_refresh_usercode_cache(self) -> None:
   587→        """Refresh usercode cache from the device."""
   588→        try:
   589→            await self.node.async_refresh_cc_values(CommandClass.USER_CODE)
   590→        except Exception as err:
   591→            raise LockDisconnected from err
   592→
   593→    async def async_get_usercodes(self) -> dict[int, int | str]:
   594→        """Get dictionary of code slots and usercodes."""
   595→        code_slots = {
   596→            int(code_slot)
   597→            for entry in self.hass.config_entries.async_entries(DOMAIN)
   598→            for code_slot in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_SLOTS, {})
   599→            if self.lock.entity_id in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_LOCKS, [])
   600→        }
   601→        data: dict[int, int | str] = {}
   602→
   603→        if not await self.async_is_connection_up():
   604→            raise LockDisconnected
   605→
   606→        slots = self._get_usercodes_from_cache()
   607→        slots_by_num = {int(slot["code_slot"]): slot for slot in slots}
   608→
   609→        # If any configured slot is missing or has unknown state, do one hard
   610→        # refresh to populate the cache. This is more efficient than fetching
   611→        # individual slots and uses Z-Wave JS's checksum optimization.
   612→        # Note: We call _async_refresh_usercode_cache directly here to avoid
   613→        # recursion since async_hard_refresh_codes calls async_get_usercodes.
   614→        if any(
   615→            slot_num not in slots_by_num or slots_by_num[slot_num].get("in_use") is None
   616→            for slot_num in code_slots
   617→        ):
   618→            _LOGGER.debug(
   619→                "Lock %s has missing/unknown slots, performing hard refresh",
   620→                self.lock.entity_id,
   621→            )
   622→            await self._async_refresh_usercode_cache()
   623→            slots = self._get_usercodes_from_cache()
   624→            slots_by_num = {int(slot["code_slot"]): slot for slot in slots}
   625→
   626→        for slot in slots:
   627→            code_slot = int(slot["code_slot"])
   628→            usercode: str = slot["usercode"] or ""
   629→            in_use: bool | None = slot["in_use"]
   630→
   631→            if not in_use:
   632→                data[code_slot] = ""
   633→            # Resolve PIN (handles masked codes), skip if unresolvable
   634→            elif resolved := self._resolve_pin_if_masked(usercode, code_slot):
   635→                data[code_slot] = resolved
   636→
   637→        slots_with_pin = [s for s, v in data.items() if v]
   638→        slots_not_enabled = [s for s, v in data.items() if not v]
   639→        _LOGGER.debug(
   640→            "Lock %s: %s slots with PIN %s, %s slots not enabled %s",
   641→            self.lock.entity_id,
   642→            len(slots_with_pin),
   643→            slots_with_pin,
   644→            len(slots_not_enabled),
   645→            slots_not_enabled,
   646→        )
   647→        return data
   648→

    51→@dataclass(repr=False, eq=False)
    52→class BaseLock:
    53→    """
    54→    Base class for lock provider implementations.
    55→
    56→    Data Fetching Modes
    57→    -------------------
    58→    The coordinator supports three update modes. All modes include an initial poll.
    59→
    60→    1. Poll for updates (default):
    61→       - Periodic calls to get_usercodes() at usercode_scan_interval
    62→       - Used when supports_push = False
    63→       - Suitable for integrations without real-time events
    64→
    65→    2. Push for updates:
    66→       - Real-time value updates via subscribe_push_updates()
    67→       - Enabled when supports_push = True
    68→       - Disables periodic polling (poll for updates)
    69→       - Updates pushed via coordinator.push_update({slot: value})
    70→
    71→    3. Poll for drift:
    72→       - Periodic hard_refresh_codes() at hard_refresh_interval
    73→       - Detects out-of-band changes (e.g., codes changed at keypad)
    74→       - Runs regardless of push/poll mode
    75→       - Set hard_refresh_interval = None to disable
    76→
    77→    4. Poll connection state:
    78→       - Periodic async_internal_is_connection_up() at connection_check_interval
    79→       - Helps detect reconnects for integrations without config entry state signals
    80→       - Set connection_check_interval = None to disable
    81→
    82→    Configuration Examples
    83→    ----------------------
    84→    Poll-only (default):
    85→        supports_push = False
    86→        usercode_scan_interval = timedelta(minutes=1)
    87→        hard_refresh_interval = None
    88→        connection_check_interval = timedelta(seconds=30)
    89→
    90→    Push with drift detection (recommended for Z-Wave JS):
    91→        supports_push = True
    92→        hard_refresh_interval = timedelta(hours=1)
    93→        connection_check_interval = None
    94→        # Override subscribe_push_updates() to handle value events
    95→
    96→    Poll with drift detection:
    97→        supports_push = False
    98→        usercode_scan_interval = timedelta(minutes=1)
    99→        hard_refresh_interval = timedelta(hours=1)
   100→        connection_check_interval = timedelta(seconds=30)
   101→
   102→    Exception Handling
   103→    ------------------
   104→    Provider implementations should raise LockCodeManagerError (or subclasses like
   105→    LockDisconnected) for lock communication failures. The coordinator catches
   106→    LockCodeManagerError and handles it appropriately (e.g., retrying, logging).
   107→
   108→    Do NOT raise generic exceptions or HomeAssistantError directly - always use
   109→    LCM-derived exceptions so the coordinator can distinguish lock failures from
   110→    other errors.
   111→    """
   112→
   113→    hass: HomeAssistant = field(repr=False)
   114→    dev_reg: dr.DeviceRegistry = field(repr=False)
   115→    ent_reg: er.EntityRegistry = field(repr=False)

   120→    _aio_lock: asyncio.Lock = field(default_factory=asyncio.Lock, init=False)
   121→    _last_operation_time: float = field(default=0.0, init=False)
   122→    _min_operation_delay: float = field(default=MIN_OPERATION_DELAY, init=False)
   123→    _last_connection_up: bool | None = field(default=None, init=False)
   124→    _config_entry_state_unsub: Callable[[], None] | None = field(
   125→        default=None, init=False

   128→
   129→    async def _async_executor_call(
   130→        self, func: Callable[..., Any], *args: Any, **kwargs: Any
   131→    ) -> Any:
   132→        """Run a sync method in the executor."""
   133→        if kwargs:
   134→            return await self.hass.async_add_executor_job(
   135→                functools.partial(func, *args, **kwargs)
   136→            )
   137→        return await self.hass.async_add_executor_job(func, *args)
   138→
   139→    async def _execute_rate_limited(
   140→        self,
   141→        operation_type: Literal["get", "set", "clear", "refresh"],
   142→        func: Callable[..., Awaitable[Any]],
   143→        *args: Any,
   144→        **kwargs: Any,
   145→    ) -> Any:
   146→        """Execute operation with connection check, serialization, and delay."""
   147→        if not await self.async_internal_is_connection_up():
   148→            raise LockDisconnected(
   149→                f"Cannot {_OPERATION_MESSAGES[operation_type]} {self.lock.entity_id} - lock not connected"
   150→            )
   151→
   152→        async with self._aio_lock:
   153→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - self._last_operation_time
   154→            if elapsed < self._min_operation_delay:
   155→                delay = self._min_operation_delay - elapsed
   156→                LOGGER.debug(
   157→                    "Rate limiting %s operation on %s, waiting %.1f seconds",
   158→                    operation_type,
   159→                    self.lock.entity_id,
   160→                    delay,
   161→                )
   162→                await asyncio.sleep(delay)
   163→
   164→            LOGGER.debug(
   165→                "Executing %s operation on %s",
   166→                operation_type,
   167→                self.lock.entity_id,
   168→            )
   169→
   170→            result = await func(*args, **kwargs)
   171→            self._last_operation_time = time.monotonic()
   172→            return result

   232→        return timedelta(seconds=30)
   233→
   234→    @property
   235→    def supports_push(self) -> bool:
   236→        """
   237→        Return whether this lock supports push-based updates.
   238→
   239→        When True, the lock will receive real-time value updates via
   240→        subscribe_push_updates() instead of periodic polling. Polling is
   241→        still used for initial load and drift detection (hard_refresh_interval).
   242→        """
   243→        return False
   244→
   245→    @property
   246→    def supports_code_slot_events(self) -> bool:

   248→        Return whether this lock supports code slot events.
   249→
   250→        When True, the lock can fire events indicating which code slot was used

   253→
   254→        Locks that don't support this will be listed in the unsupported_locks

   258→
   259→    @callback
   260→    def subscribe_push_updates(self) -> None:

   262→        Subscribe to push-based value updates.

   264→        Override in subclasses that support push. Called during async_setup()

   268→        Implementations MUST be idempotent (no-op if already subscribed).
   269→        """
   270→        self._raise_not_implemented(
   271→            "subscribe_push_updates",
   272→            "Override this method to subscribe to real-time value updates "
   273→            "and call coordinator.push_update({slot: value}) when updates arrive. "
   274→            "Must be idempotent (no-op if already subscribed).",

   276→

   287→        self._raise_not_implemented(

   291→        )

   294→        """Set up lock."""
   295→        pass
   296→
   297→    async def async_setup(self, config_entry: ConfigEntry) -> None:
   298→        """Set up lock and coordinator."""
   299→        await self.hass.async_add_executor_job(self.setup)
   300→
   301→        lock_entity_id = self.lock.entity_id
   302→        # Track the provider's config entry (e.g., zwave_js) so we can resubscribe
   303→        # when that integration reloads or reconnects.
   304→        self._setup_config_entry_state_listener()
   305→
   306→        # Reuse existing coordinator or create new one
   307→        if self.coordinator is not None:
   308→            self.hass.async_create_task(
   309→                self.coordinator.async_request_refresh(),
   310→                f"Refresh coordinator for {lock_entity_id}",
   311→            )
   312→            return
   313→
   314→        self.coordinator = LockUsercodeUpdateCoordinator(self.hass, self, config_entry)
   315→        if config_entry.state == ConfigEntryState.SETUP_IN_PROGRESS:
   316→            try:
   317→                await self.coordinator.async_config_entry_first_refresh()
   318→            except (ConfigEntryNotReady, UpdateFailed) as err:
   319→                LOGGER.warning(
   320→                    "Failed to fetch initial data for lock %s: %s. "
   321→                    "Entities will be created but unavailable until lock is ready.",
   322→                    lock_entity_id,
   323→                    err,
   324→                )
   325→        else:
   326→            await self.coordinator.async_refresh()
   327→            if not self.coordinator.last_update_success:
   328→                LOGGER.warning(
   329→                    "Failed to fetch initial data for lock %s: %s. "
   330→                    "Entities will be created but unavailable until lock is ready.",
   331→                    lock_entity_id,
   332→                    self.coordinator.last_exception,
   333→                )
   334→
   335→        # Subscribe to push updates after coordinator is ready. If the provider's
   336→        # config entry isn't loaded yet, defer and let the state listener resubscribe.
   337→        if self.supports_push:
   338→            if (
   339→                self.lock_config_entry
   340→                and self.lock_config_entry.state != ConfigEntryState.LOADED
   341→            ):
   342→                LOGGER.debug(
   343→                    "Lock %s: deferring push subscription until config entry is loaded",
   344→                    lock_entity_id,
   345→                )
   346→            else:
   347→                self.subscribe_push_updates()
   348→
   349→    def unload(self, remove_permanently: bool) -> None:
   350→        """Unload lock."""
   351→        pass
   352→
   353→    async def async_unload(self, remove_permanently: bool) -> None:
   354→        """Unload lock."""
   355→        if self._config_entry_state_unsub:
   356→            self._config_entry_state_unsub()
   357→            self._config_entry_state_unsub = None
   358→
   359→        # Unsubscribe from push updates before unloading
   360→        if self.supports_push:
   361→            self.unsubscribe_push_updates()
   362→
   363→        await self.hass.async_add_executor_job(self.unload, remove_permanently)
   364→
   365→    def is_connection_up(self) -> bool:
   366→        """Return whether connection to lock is up."""
   367→        raise NotImplementedError()
   368→
   369→    def _setup_config_entry_state_listener(self) -> None:
   370→        """Listen for provider config entry state changes to resubscribe."""
   371→        lock_entry = self.lock_config_entry
   372→        if not lock_entry or self._config_entry_state_unsub:
   373→            return
   374→
   375→        self._last_entry_state = lock_entry.state
   376→
   377→        @callback
   378→        def _handle_state_change() -> None:
   379→            to_state = lock_entry.state
   380→            if to_state == self._last_entry_state:
   381→                return
   382→
   383→            if to_state == ConfigEntryState.LOADED:
   384→                if self.coordinator:
   385→                    self.hass.async_create_task(
   386→                        self.coordinator.async_request_refresh(),
   387→                        f"Refresh coordinator for {self.lock.entity_id} after reload",
   388→                    )
   389→                if self.supports_push:
   390→                    self.subscribe_push_updates()
   391→            elif (
   392→                self.supports_push and self._last_entry_state == ConfigEntryState.LOADED
   393→            ):
   394→                self.unsubscribe_push_updates()
   395→
   396→            self._last_entry_state = to_state
   397→
   398→        self._config_entry_state_unsub = lock_entry.async_on_state_change(
   399→            _handle_state_change
   400→        )
   401→
   402→    async def async_is_connection_up(self) -> bool:
   403→        """Return whether connection to lock is up."""
   404→        return await self._async_executor_call(self.is_connection_up)
   405→
   406→    @final
   407→    async def async_internal_is_connection_up(self) -> bool:
   408→        """Return whether connection to lock is up."""
   409→        is_up = await self.async_is_connection_up()
   410→        lock_entry = self.lock_config_entry
   411→        if self.supports_push and lock_entry:
   412→            # Only react to connection transitions when the config entry is loaded.
   413→            if lock_entry.state == ConfigEntryState.LOADED:
   414→                if self._last_connection_up is False and is_up:
   415→                    if self.coordinator:
   416→                        self.hass.async_create_task(
   417→                            self.coordinator.async_request_refresh(),
   418→                            f"Refresh coordinator for {self.lock.entity_id} after reconnect",
   419→                        )
   420→                    self.subscribe_push_updates()
   421→                elif self._last_connection_up is True and not is_up:
   422→                    self.unsubscribe_push_updates()
   423→        self._last_connection_up = is_up
   424→        return is_up

   463→    def set_usercode(
   464→        self, code_slot: int, usercode: int | str, name: str | None = None
   465→    ) -> bool:
   466→        """
   467→        Set a usercode on a code slot.
   468→
   469→        Returns True if the value was changed, False if already set to this value.
   470→        If the provider cannot determine whether a change occurred, return True
   471→        to ensure the coordinator refreshes and verifies the state.
   472→
   473→        Optimistic Updates
   474→        ------------------
   475→        For providers where the underlying integration's value cache updates
   476→        asynchronously (e.g., Z-Wave JS push notifications), the base class
   477→        coordinator refresh may read stale data, causing sync loops.
   478→
   479→        In these cases, call ``self.coordinator.push_update({code_slot: usercode})``
   480→        after a successful set operation to update coordinator data immediately.
   481→        This prevents the sync sensor from seeing a mismatch and retrying.
   482→
   483→        Providers with synchronous caches (like Virtual) don't need this as
   484→        get_usercodes() returns the updated value immediately.
   485→
   486→        Raises:
   487→            LockDisconnected: If the lock cannot be communicated with.
   488→
   489→        """
   490→        self._raise_not_implemented(
   491→            "set_usercode",
   492→            "Override this method to set a usercode on the lock.",
   493→        )
   494→
   495→    async def async_set_usercode(
   496→        self, code_slot: int, usercode: int | str, name: str | None = None
   497→    ) -> bool:
   498→        """
   499→        Set a usercode on a code slot.
   500→
   501→        Returns True if the value was changed, False if already set to this value.
   502→        If the provider cannot determine whether a change occurred, return True
   503→        to ensure the coordinator refreshes and verifies the state.
   504→        """
   505→        return await self._async_executor_call(
   506→            self.set_usercode, code_slot, usercode, name=name
   507→        )
   508→
   509→    @final
   510→    async def async_internal_set_usercode(
   511→        self, code_slot: int, usercode: int | str, name: str | None = None
   512→    ) -> None:
   513→        """Set a usercode on a code slot."""
   514→        changed = await self._execute_rate_limited(
   515→            "set", self.async_set_usercode, code_slot, usercode, name=name
   516→        )
   517→        # Refresh coordinator to update entity states from cache (only if changed)
   518→        if changed and self.coordinator:
   519→            await self.coordinator.async_request_refresh()

   624→
   625→    @final
   626→    def call_service(
   627→        self,
   628→        domain: str,
   629→        service: str,
   630→        service_data: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   631→        blocking: bool = True,
   632→    ):
   633→        """Call a hass service and log a failure on an error."""
   634→        try:
   635→            self.hass.services.call(
   636→                domain, service, service_data=service_data, blocking=blocking
   637→            )
   638→        except Exception as err:
   639→            LOGGER.error(
   640→                "Error calling %s.%s service call: %s", domain, service, str(err)
   641→            )
   642→
   643→    @final
   644→    async def async_call_service(
   645→        self,
   646→        domain: str,
   647→        service: str,
   648→        service_data: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   649→        blocking: bool = True,
   650→    ):
   651→        """Call a hass service and re-raise failures as LockDisconnected."""
   652→        try:
   653→            await self.hass.services.async_call(
   654→                domain, service, service_data=service_data, blocking=blocking
   655→            )
   656→        except Exception as err:
   657→            LOGGER.error(
   658→                "Error calling %s.%s service call: %s", domain, service, str(err)
   659→            )
   660→            raise LockDisconnected(
   661→                f"Service call {domain}.{service} failed: {err}"
   662→            ) from err
   663→
   664→    @final
   665→    @callback
   666→    def async_fire_code_slot_event(
   667→        self,
   668→        code_slot: int | None = None,
   669→        to_locked: bool | None = None,
   670→        action_text: str | None = None,
   671→        source_data: Event | State | dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   672→    ) -> None:
   673→        """
   674→        Fire a code slot event.
   675→
   676→        Sub-classes should call this whenever a code slot is used. source_data can
   677→        include any data that is JSON serializable if the source is not a Home
   678→        Assistant event or state.
   679→        """
   680→        name_state: State | None = None
   681→        lock_entity_id = self.lock.entity_id
   682→        lock_device_id = self.lock.device_id
   683→        config_entry_id: str | None = None
   684→
   685→        try:
   686→            config_entry = next(
   687→                config_entry
   688→                for config_entry in self.hass.config_entries.async_entries(DOMAIN)
   689→                if (
   690→                    self.lock.entity_id in get_entry_data(config_entry, CONF_LOCKS, [])
   691→                    and code_slot is not None
   692→                    and int(code_slot)
   693→                    in (
   694→                        int(slot)
   695→                        for slot in get_entry_data(config_entry, CONF_SLOTS, {})
   696→                    )
   697→                    and (
   698→                        name_entity_id := self.ent_reg.async_get_entity_id(
   699→                            TEXT_DOMAIN,
   700→                            DOMAIN,
   701→                            f"{config_entry.entry_id}|{code_slot}|{CONF_NAME}",
   702→                        )
   703→                    )
   704→                )
   705→            )
   706→        except StopIteration:
   707→            pass
   708→        else:
   709→            config_entry_id = config_entry.entry_id
   710→            name_state = self.hass.states.get(name_entity_id)
   711→
   712→        from_state: str | None = None
   713→        to_state: str | None = None
   714→        if to_locked:
   715→            from_state = LockState.UNLOCKED
   716→            to_state = LockState.LOCKED
   717→        elif to_locked is False:
   718→            from_state = LockState.LOCKED
   719→            to_state = LockState.UNLOCKED
   720→
   721→        notification_source: Literal["event", "state"] | None = None
   722→        extra_data: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   723→        if isinstance(source_data, Event):
   724→            notification_source = "event"
   725→            extra_data = {
   726→                "event_type": source_data.event_type,
   727→                "data": source_data.data,
   728→                "time_fired": source_data.time_fired.isoformat(),
   729→            }
   730→        elif isinstance(source_data, State):
   731→            notification_source = "state"
   732→            last_changed_isoformat = source_data.last_changed.isoformat()
   733→            if source_data.last_changed == source_data.last_updated:
   734→                last_updated_isoformat = last_changed_isoformat
   735→            else:
   736→                last_updated_isoformat = source_data.last_updated.isoformat()
   737→            extra_data = {
   738→                "entity_id": source_data.entity_id,
   739→                "state": source_data.state,
   740→                "attributes": source_data.attributes,
   741→                "last_changed": last_changed_isoformat,
   742→                "last_updated": last_updated_isoformat,
   743→            }
   744→        elif isinstance(source_data, dict):
   745→            extra_data = source_data
   746→
   747→        event_data = {
   748→            ATTR_NOTIFICATION_SOURCE: notification_source,
   749→            ATTR_ENTITY_ID: lock_entity_id,
   750→            ATTR_DEVICE_ID: lock_device_id,
   751→            ATTR_LCM_CONFIG_ENTRY_ID: config_entry_id,
   752→            ATTR_STATE: (
   753→                state.state if (state := self.hass.states.get(lock_entity_id)) else ""
   754→            ),
   755→            ATTR_ACTION_TEXT: action_text,
   756→            ATTR_CODE_SLOT: code_slot or 0,
   757→            ATTR_CODE_SLOT_NAME: name_state.state if name_state else "",
   758→            ATTR_FROM: from_state,
   759→            ATTR_TO: to_state,
   760→            ATTR_EXTRA_DATA: extra_data,
   761→        }
   762→
   763→        if self.lock_config_entry:
   764→            event_data[ATTR_LOCK_CONFIG_ENTRY_ID] = self.lock_config_entry.entry_id
   765→
   766→        self.hass.bus.async_fire(EVENT_LOCK_STATE_CHANGED, event_data=event_data)
   767→

   856→async def test_notification_event_keypad_lock_fires_lock_state_changed(
   857→    hass: HomeAssistant,

   862→    """Test that a keypad lock notification event fires EVENT_LOCK_STATE_CHANGED."""
   863→    lcm_entry = MockConfigEntry(domain=DOMAIN, data={CONF_LOCKS: [], CONF_SLOTS: {}})
   864→    lcm_entry.add_to_hass(hass)

   884→                "eventLabel": "Keypad lock operation",
   885→                "parameters": {"userId": 1},
   886→            },
   887→        },
   888→    )
   889→    lock_schlage_be469.receive_event(event)
   890→    await hass.async_block_till_done()
   891→
   892→    # Verify the LCM event was fired

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/raman/projects/lock_code_manager/custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py",
  "old_string": "    @property\n    def domain(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Return integration domain.\"\"\"\n        return ZWAVE_JS_DOMAIN",
  "new_string": "    @callback\n    def _handle_duplicate_code(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Handle duplicate code rejection from the lock.\"\"\"\n        code_slot = self._set_in_progress_code_slot\n        self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None\n\n        _LOGGER.error(\n            \"Lock %s rejected code for slot %s because it duplicates a code \"\n            \"in another slot. The slot has been disabled. Please set a unique \"\n            \"code and re-enable the slot\",\n            self.lock.entity_id,\n            code_slot,\n        )\n\n        for entry in self.hass.config_entries.async_entries(DOMAIN):\n            if self.lock.entity_id not in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_LOCKS, []):\n                continue\n            slots = get_entry_data(entry, CONF_SLOTS, {})\n            slot_key = str(code_slot)\n            if slot_key not in slots:\n                continue\n\n            # Disable the slot in the config entry\n            new_data = copy.deepcopy(dict(entry.data))\n            new_data[CONF_SLOTS][slot_key][CONF_ENABLED] = False\n            self.hass.config_entries.async_update_entry(entry, data=new_data)\n\n            async_create(\n                self.hass,\n                (\n                    f\"Lock **{self.lock.entity_id}** rejected the code for \"\n                    f\"slot **{code_slot}** because it duplicates a code in \"\n                    f\"another slot. The slot has been disabled. Please set a \"\n                    f\"unique code and re-enable the slot.\"\n                ),\n                title=\"Duplicate Lock Code Rejected\",\n                notification_id=f\"{DOMAIN}_{entry.entry_id}_{code_slot}_duplicate_code\",\n            )\n\n    @property\n    def domain(self) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Return integration domain.\"\"\"\n        return ZWAVE_JS_DOMAIN"
}

Fire event 15 without userId in parameters\n    event = ZwaveEvent(\n        type=\"notification\",\n        data={\n            \"source\": \"node\",\n            \"event\": \"notification\",\n            \"nodeId\": lock_schlage_be469.node_id,\n            \"endpointIndex\": 0,\n            \"ccId\": 113,\n            \"args\": {\n                \"type\": 6,\n                \"event\": 15,\n                \"label\": \"Access Control\",\n                \"eventLabel\": \"New user code not added due to duplicate code\",\n                \"parameters\": {},\n            },\n        },\n    )\n    lock_schlage_be469.receive_event(event)\n    await hass.async_block_till_done()\n\n    # Slot should be disabled\n    assert lcm_entry.data[CONF_SLOTS][\"3\"][CONF_ENABLED] is False\n    assert zwave_js_lock._set_in_progress_code_slot is None\n\n    await zwave_js_lock.async_unload(False)\n\n\nasync def test_duplicate_code_notification_ignored_when_not_in_progress(\n    hass: HomeAssistant,\n    zwave_js_lock: ZWaveJSLock,\n    zwave_integration: MockConfigEntry,\n    lock_schlage_be469: Node,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Test event 15 is ignored when _set_in_progress_code_slot is None.\"\"\"\n    lcm_entry = MockConfigEntry(\n        domain=DOMAIN,\n        data={\n            CONF_LOCKS: [zwave_js_lock.lock.entity_id],\n            CONF_SLOTS: {\"2\": {CONF_ENABLED: True}},\n        },\n    )\n    lcm_entry.add_to_hass(hass)\n    await zwave_js_lock.async_setup(lcm_entry)\n\n    # Do NOT set _set_in_progress_code_slot (external trigger)\n    assert zwave_js_lock._set_in_progress_code_slot is None\n\n    # Fire event 15\n    event = ZwaveEvent(\n        type=\"notification\",\n        data={\n            \"source\": \"node\",\n            \"event\": \"notification\",\n            \"nodeId\": lock_schlage_be469.node_id,\n            \"endpointIndex\": 0,\n            \"ccId\": 113,\n            \"args\": {\n                \"type\": 6,\n                \"event\": 15,\n                \"label\": \"Access Control\",\n                \"eventLabel\": \"New user code not added due to duplicate code\",\n                \"parameters\": {\"userId\": 2},\n            },\n        },\n    )\n    lock_schlage_be469.receive_event(event)\n    await hass.async_block_till_done()\n\n    # Slot should NOT be disabled (still True)\n    assert lcm_entry.data[CONF_SLOTS][\"2\"][CONF_ENABLED] is True\n\n    # No persistent notification\n    notifications = _async_get_or_create_notifications(hass)\n    notification_id = f\"{DOMAIN}_{lcm_entry.entry_id}_2_duplicate_code\"\n    assert notification_id not in notifications\n\n    await zwave_js_lock.async_unload(False)\n\n\nasync def test_duplicate_code_notification_ignored_when_user_id_mismatches(\n    hass: HomeAssistant,\n    zwave_js_lock: ZWaveJSLock,\n    zwave_integration: MockConfigEntry,\n    lock_schlage_be469: Node,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Test event 15 is ignored when userId doesn't match in-progress slot.\"\"\"\n    lcm_entry = MockConfigEntry(\n        domain=DOMAIN,\n        data={\n            CONF_LOCKS: [zwave_js_lock.lock.entity_id],\n            CONF_SLOTS: {\"2\": {CONF_ENABLED: True}, \"3\": {CONF_ENABLED: True}},\n        },\n    )\n    lcm_entry.add_to_hass(hass)\n    await zwave_js_lock.async_setup(lcm_entry)\n\n    # Neither slot should be disabled\n    assert lcm_entry.data[CONF_SLOTS][\"2\"][CONF_ENABLED] is True\n    assert lcm_entry.data[CONF_SLOTS][\"3\"][CONF_ENABLED] is True\n\n    # In-progress should NOT be cleared (it wasn't our event)\n    assert zwave_js_lock._set_in_progress_code_slot == 2\n\n    await zwave_js_lock.async_unload(False)\n\n\nasync def test_set_in_progress_cleared_on_value_update(\n    hass: HomeAssistant,\n    zwave_js_lock: ZWaveJSLock,\n    zwave_integration: MockConfigEntry,\n    lock_schlage_be469: Node,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Test that a push value update for the in-progress slot clears the field.\"\"\"\n    lcm_entry = MockConfigEntry(\n        domain=DOMAIN,\n        data={\n            CONF_LOCKS: [zwave_js_lock.lock.entity_id],\n            CONF_SLOTS: {\"2\": {}},\n        },\n    )\n    lcm_entry.add_to_hass(hass)\n    await zwave_js_lock.async_setup(lcm_entry)\n\n    mock_coordinator = MagicMock()\n    mock_coordinator.data = {2: \"\"}\n    zwave_js_lock.coordinator = mock_coordinator\n\n    zwave_js_lock.subscribe_push_updates()\n\n    # Simulate LCM setting a code on slot 2\n    zwave_js_lock._set_in_progress_code_slot = 2\n\n    # Simulate a push value update for slot 2 (lock accepted the code)\n    event = ZwaveEvent(\n        type=\"value updated\",\n        data={\n            \"args\": {\n                \"commandClass\": CommandClass.USER_CODE,\n                \"property\": LOCK_USERCODE_PROPERTY,\n                \"propertyKey\": 2,\n                \"newValue\": \"1234\",\n            },\n        },\n    )\n    lock_schlage_be469.emit(\"value updated\", event.data)\n    await hass.async_block_till_done()\n\n    # The slot has been disabled. Please set a \"\n                    f\"unique code and re-enable the slot.\"\n                ),\n                title=\"Duplicate Lock Code Rejected\",\n                notification_id=f\"{DOMAIN}_{entry.entry_id}_{code_slot}_duplicate_code\",\n            )",
  "new_string": "    @callback\n    def _handle_duplicate_code(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Handle duplicate code rejection from the lock.\"\"\"\n        code_slot = self._set_in_progress_code_slot\n        self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None\n\n        affected_entries: list[str] = []\n        slot_key = str(code_slot)\n        for entry in self.hass.config_entries.async_entries(DOMAIN):\n            if self.lock.entity_id not in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_LOCKS, []):\n                continue\n            slots = get_entry_data(entry, CONF_SLOTS, {})\n            if slot_key not in slots:\n                continue\n\n            # Disable the slot in the config entry\n            new_data = copy.deepcopy(dict(entry.data))\n            new_data[CONF_SLOTS][slot_key][CONF_ENABLED] = False\n            self.hass.config_entries.async_update_entry(entry, data=new_data)\n            affected_entries.append(entry.title or entry.entry_id)\n\n        _LOGGER.error(\n            \"Lock %s rejected code for slot %s because it duplicates a code \"\n            \"in another slot. The slot has been disabled in %s. Please set a \"\n            \"unique code and re-enable the slot\",\n            self.lock.entity_id,\n            code_slot,\n            \", \".join(affected_entries) if affected_entries else \"no config entries\",\n        )\n\n        if affected_entries:\n            entries_text = \", \".join(f\"**{e}**\" for e in affected_entries)\n            async_create(\n                self.hass,\n                (\n                    f\"Lock **{self.lock.entity_id}** rejected the code for \"\n                    f\"slot **{code_slot}** because it duplicates a code in \"\n                    f\"another slot. The slot has been disabled in {entries_text}. \"\n                    f\"Please set a unique code and re-enable the slot.\"\n                ),\n                title=\"Duplicate Lock Code Rejected\",\n                notification_id=(\n                    f\"{DOMAIN}_{self.lock.entity_id}_{code_slot}_duplicate_code\"\n                ),\n            )"
}

> AGENT

Here's a summary of what was implemented:

### Changes to `providers/zwave_js.py`

1. **New field** `_set_in_progress_code_slot: int | None` on `ZWaveJSLock` — tracks which slot LCM is currently setting a code on

2. **`async_set_usercode`** — sets `_set_in_progress_code_slot = code_slot` before the service call

3. **`on_value_updated`** (inside `subscribe_push_updates`) — clears `_set_in_progress_code_slot` when a real push update arrives for the in-progress slot, confirming the lock accepted the code

4. **`_handle_zwave_js_event`** — before the normal event flow, checks for event 15 (`NEW_USER_CODE_NOT_ADDED_DUE_TO_DUPLICATE_CODE`) but only when `_set_in_progress_code_slot` is set and the userId matches (or is absent)

5. **New `_handle_duplicate_code()` method** — for each matching config entry: disables the slot via `async_update_entry`, collects entry titles. Then logs one error and creates one lock-specific persistent notification (keyed by `{DOMAIN}_{lock_entity_id}_{slot}_duplicate_code`) listing all affected config entries.

### Changes to `tests/providers/test_zwave_js.py`

5 new tests:
- `test_duplicate_code_notification_disables_slot` — matching userId, slot disabled, notification created, error logged
- `test_duplicate_code_notification_no_user_id_disables_slot` — no userId, still handled via in-progress slot
- `test_duplicate_code_notification_ignored_when_not_in_progress` — no in-progress slot, event ignored
- `test_duplicate_code_notification_ignored_when_user_id_mismatches` — userId differs from in-progress, ignored
- `test_set_in_progress_cleared_on_value_update` — push value update clears the field

+    @callback
+    def _handle_duplicate_code(self) -> None:
+        """Handle duplicate code rejection from the lock."""
+        code_slot = self._set_in_progress_code_slot
+        self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None
+
+        affected_entries: list[str] = []
+        slot_key = str(code_slot)
+        for entry in self.hass.config_entries.async_entries(DOMAIN):
+            if self.lock.entity_id not in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_LOCKS, []):
+                continue
+            slots = get_entry_data(entry, CONF_SLOTS, {})
+            if slot_key not in slots:
+                continue
+
+            # Disable the slot in the config entry
+            new_data = copy.deepcopy(dict(entry.data))
+            new_data[CONF_SLOTS][slot_key][CONF_ENABLED] = False
+            self.hass.config_entries.async_update_entry(entry, data=new_data)
+            affected_entries.append(entry.title or entry.entry_id)
+
+        _LOGGER.error(
+            "Lock %s rejected code for slot %s because it duplicates a code "
+            "in another slot. The slot has been disabled in %s. Please set a "
+            "unique code and re-enable the slot",
+            self.lock.entity_id,
+            code_slot,
+            ", ".join(affected_entries) if affected_entries else "no config entries",
+        )
+
+        if affected_entries:
+            entries_text = ", ".join(f"**{e}**" for e in affected_entries)
+            async_create(
+                self.hass,
+                (
+                    f"Lock **{self.lock.entity_id}** rejected the code for "
+                    f"slot **{code_slot}** because it duplicates a code in "
+                    f"another slot. The slot has been disabled in {entries_text}. "
+                    f"Please set a unique code and re-enable the slot."
+                ),
+                title="Duplicate Lock Code Rejected",
+                notification_id=(
+                    f"{DOMAIN}_{self.lock.entity_id}_{code_slot}_duplicate_code"
+                ),
+            )
+

```json
{
  "command": "gh pr create --title \"Handle duplicate code Z-Wave notification\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Proposed change\n\nWhen a lock rejects a code because it duplicates a code in another slot, Z-Wave sends an Access Control notification (type 6, event 15: `NEW_USER_CODE_NOT_ADDED_DUE_TO_DUPLICATE_CODE`). Previously, LCM ignored this notification, so the in-sync sensor would see the code isn't set, retry, get rejected again, and loop indefinitely.\n\nThis PR adds handling for this notification: when LCM initiated the set operation and receives event 15, it disables the affected slot in all matching config entries, logs an error, and creates a single lock-specific persistent notification listing all affected config entries so the user knows to set a unique code and re-enable the slot.\n\nKey design decisions:\n- **Guard against external triggers**: Only reacts when `_set_in_progress_code_slot` is set (i.e., LCM initiated the set), preventing false positives from codes added at the lock's keypad\n- **Lock-specific notification**: One persistent notification per lock+slot (not per config entry), listing all affected config entries in the message\n- **Field lifecycle**: `_set_in_progress_code_slot` is set before the service call, cleared on success (push value update) or failure (duplicate notification)\n\n## Type of change\n\n- [x] Bugfix (non-breaking change which fixes an issue)\n\n## Additional information\n\n- This PR fixes or closes issue: fixes #848\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Create PR using template format"
}
```

> DEVELOPER

what's simpler/more pythonic to disable the slot, updating the entry data, or calling the service on the switch?

> AGENT

Calling the service on the switch is simpler — it's the same pattern users would use, avoids `copy.deepcopy` of config entry data, and lets the existing switch logic handle state updates naturally. The config entry approach is lower-level plumbing that bypasses the normal entity layer.

Want me to refactor `_handle_duplicate_code` to use the switch service call instead?

> DEVELOPER

yes

  "new_string": "    async def _async_handle_duplicate_code(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Handle duplicate code rejection from the lock.\"\"\"\n        code_slot = self._set_in_progress_code_slot\n        self._set_in_progress_code_slot = None\n\n        # Find all enabled switch entities for this slot across config entries\n        switch_entity_ids: list[str] = []\n        affected_entries: list[str] = []\n        slot_key = str(code_slot)\n        for entry in self.hass.config_entries.async_entries(DOMAIN):\n            if self.lock.entity_id not in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_LOCKS, []):\n                continue\n            if slot_key not in get_entry_data(entry, CONF_SLOTS, {}):\n                continue\n            enabled_uid = f\"{entry.entry_id}|{code_slot}|{CONF_ENABLED}\"\n            entity_id = self.ent_reg.async_get_entity_id(\n                SWITCH_DOMAIN, DOMAIN, enabled_uid\n            )\n            if entity_id:\n                switch_entity_ids.append(entity_id)\n                affected_entries.append(entry.title or entry.entry_id)\n\n        if switch_entity_ids:\n            await asyncio.gather(\n                *(\n                    self.hass.services.async_call(\n                        SWITCH_DOMAIN,\n                        SERVICE_TURN_OFF,\n                        {ATTR_ENTITY_ID: entity_id},\n                        blocking=True,\n                    )\n                    for entity_id in switch_entity_ids\n                )\n            )\n\n        _LOGGER.error(\n            \"Lock %s rejected code for slot %s because it duplicates a code \"\n            \"in another slot. The slot has been disabled. Please set a unique \"\n            \"code and re-enable the slot\",\n            self.lock.entity_id,\n            code_slot,\n        )\n\n        async_create(\n            self.hass,\n            (\n                f\"Lock **{self.lock.entity_id}** rejected the code for \"\n                f\"slot **{code_slot}** because it duplicates a code in \"\n                f\"another slot. The slot has been disabled. \"\n                f\"Please set a unique code and re-enable the slot.\"\n            ),\n            title=\"Duplicate Lock Code Rejected\",\n            notification_id=(\n                f\"{DOMAIN}_{self.lock.entity_id}_{code_slot}_duplicate_code\"\n            ),\n        )"
}

The tests need real switch entities for the service call to work. Since these are LCM switch entities that get created during full setup, and our tests use a lightweight `zwave_js_lock` fixture without full LCM platform setup, I should mock the service call instead. That's simpler and tests the right thing — that `_async_handle_duplicate_code` calls `switch.turn_off` on the right entities.

Let me rewrite all 5 tests:

    57→@dataclass(repr=False, eq=False)
    58→class MockLCMLock(BaseLock):
    59→    """Mock Lock Code Manager lock instance."""
    60→
    61→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    62→        """Initialize mock lock."""
    63→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    64→        self._connected = True
    65→
    66→    @property
    67→    def domain(self) -> str:
    68→        """Return integration domain."""
    69→        return "test"
    70→
    71→    @callback
    72→    def setup(self) -> None:
    73→        """Set up lock."""
    74→        self.hass.data.setdefault(LOCK_DATA, {}).setdefault(
    75→            self.lock.entity_id,
    76→            {"codes": {1: "1234", 2: "5678"}, "service_calls": defaultdict(list)},
    77→        )
    78→
    79→    @callback
    80→    def unload(self, remove_permanently: bool) -> None:
    81→        """Unload lock."""
    82→        self.hass.data[LOCK_DATA].pop(self.lock.entity_id)
    83→        if not self.hass.data[LOCK_DATA]:
    84→            self.hass.data.pop(LOCK_DATA)
    85→
    86→    def set_connected(self, connected: bool) -> None:
    87→        """Set connection state for testing."""
    88→        self._connected = connected
    89→
    90→    def is_connection_up(self) -> bool:
    91→        """Return whether connection to lock is up."""
    92→        return self._connected
    93→
    94→    def hard_refresh_codes(self) -> None:
    95→        """
    96→        Perform hard refresh all codes.
    97→
    98→        Needed for integraitons where usercodes are cached and may get out of sync with
    99→        the lock.
   100→        """
   101→        self.hass.data[LOCK_DATA][self.lock.entity_id]["service_calls"][
   102→            "hard_refresh_codes"
   103→        ].append(())
   104→
   105→    def set_usercode(
   106→        self, code_slot: int, usercode: int | str, name: str | None = None
   107→    ) -> bool:
   108→        """
   109→        Set a usercode on a code slot.
   110→
   111→        Returns True if the value was changed, False if already set to this value.
   112→        """
   113→        lock_data = self.hass.data.get(LOCK_DATA, {}).get(self.lock.entity_id)
   114→        if lock_data:
   115→            # Check if value already matches
   116→            if lock_data["codes"].get(code_slot) == usercode:
   117→                return False
   118→            lock_data["codes"][code_slot] = usercode
   119→            lock_data["service_calls"]["set_usercode"].append(
   120→                (code_slot, usercode, name)
   121→            )
   122→            return True
   123→        return True
   124→
   125→    def clear_usercode(self, code_slot: int) -> bool:
   126→        """
   127→        Clear a usercode on a code slot.
   128→
   129→        Returns True if the value was changed, False if already cleared.
   130→        """
   131→        lock_data = self.hass.data.get(LOCK_DATA, {}).get(self.lock.entity_id)
   132→        if lock_data:
   133→            if code_slot not in lock_data["codes"]:
   134→                return False
   135→            lock_data["codes"].pop(code_slot, None)
   136→            lock_data["service_calls"]["clear_usercode"].append((code_slot,))
   137→            return True
   138→        return True
   139→
   140→    def get_usercodes(self) -> dict[int, int | str]:
   141→        """
   142→        Get dictionary of code slots and usercodes.
   143→
   144→        Called by data coordinator to get data for code slot sensors.
   145→
   146→        Key is code slot, value is usercode, e.g.:
   147→        {
   148→            1: '1234',
   149→            'B': '5678',
   150→        }
   151→        """
   152→        lock_data = self.hass.data.get(LOCK_DATA, {}).get(self.lock.entity_id)
   153→        if not lock_data:
   154→            return {}
   155→        codes = lock_data["codes"]
   156→        lock_data["service_calls"]["get_usercodes"].append(codes)
   157→        return codes
   158→
   159→
   160→class MockLockEntity(LockEntity):
   161→    """Mocked lock entity."""
   162→
   163→    _attr_has_entity_name = True
   164→
   165→    def __init__(self, name: str) -> None:
   166→        """Initialize the lock."""
   167→        self._attr_name = name
   168→        self._attr_unique_id = slugify(name)
   169→        self._attr_is_locked = False
   170→        self._attr_has_entity_name = False
   171→        self._attr_device_info = DeviceInfo(

   177→class MockCalendarEntity(CalendarEntity):
   178→    """Test Calendar entity."""
   179→
   180→    _attr_has_entity_name = True
   181→
   182→    def __init__(self, name: str, events: list[CalendarEvent] | None = None) -> None:
   183→        """Initialize entity."""
   184→        self._attr_name = name.capitalize()
   185→        self._events = events or []
   186→
   187→        self._attr_unique_id = slugify(name)
   188→
   189→    @property
   190→    def event(self) -> CalendarEvent | None:
   191→        """Return the next upcoming event."""
   192→        return self._events[0] if self._events else None
   193→
   194→    @callback
   195→    def create_event(self, **kwargs) -> CalendarEvent:
   196→        """Create a new fake event, used by tests."""
   197→        event = CalendarEvent(
   198→            start=kwargs["dtstart"], end=kwargs["dtend"], summary=kwargs["summary"]
   199→        )
   200→        self._events.append(event)
   201→        self.async_write_ha_state()
   202→        return event
   203→
   204→    @callback
   205→    def delete_event(
   206→        self,
   207→        uid: str,
   208→        recurrence_id: str | None = None,
   209→        recurrence_range: str | None = None,
   210→    ) -> None:
   211→        """Delete an event on the calendar."""
   212→        for event in self._events:
   213→            if event.uid == uid:
   214→                self._events.remove(event)
   215→                self.async_write_ha_state()
   216→                return
   217→
   218→    @callback
   219→    def get_events(
   220→        self,
   221→        hass: HomeAssistant,
   222→        start_date: datetime,
   223→        end_date: datetime,
   224→    ) -> list[CalendarEvent]:
   225→        """Return calendar events within a datetime range."""
   226→        assert start_date < end_date
   227→        events = []
   228→        for event in self._events:
   229→            if event.start_datetime_local >= end_date:
   230→                continue
   231→            if event.end_datetime_local < start_date:
   232→                continue
   233→            events.append(event)
   234→        return events
   235→

I see — the full LCM setup uses `MockLCMLock` (with `test` domain), not the real `ZWaveJSLock`. The zwave_js tests use a lightweight setup. The issue is that in the zwave_js tests, LCM config entries are created with `MockConfigEntry` + `zwave_js_lock.async_setup(lcm_entry)` but the LCM platform entities (switches etc.) aren't set up because `hass.config_entries.async_setup` isn't called for the LCM entry.

So to get a full LCM setup with ZWaveJSLock, I need to:

3. Call `hass.config_entries.async_setup` on it

But actually, I think the simpler approach is: the zwave_js tests already have the lock entity via the `zwave_integration` fixture. I just need to make sure `INTEGRATIONS_CLASS_MAP` includes `zwave_js`.

lcm_entry.entry_id, 3)\n\n    from custom_components.lock_code_manager.data import LockCodeManagerConfigEntryData\n\n    runtime_data: LockCodeManagerConfigEntryData = lcm_entry.runtime_data\n    lock_instance = runtime_data.locks[zwave_js_lock.lock.entity_id]\n\n    # LCM is setting slot 2, but notification says slot 3\n    lock_instance._set_in_progress_code_slot = 2\n\n    lock_schlage_be469.receive_event(\n        _make_duplicate_code_event(lock_schlage_be469.node_id, user_id=3)\n    )\n    await hass.async_block_till_done()\n\n    # Neither switch should be turned off\n    assert hass.states.get(switch_2).state == STATE_ON\n    assert hass.states.get(switch_3).state == STATE_ON\n\n    # In-progress should NOT be cleared (it wasn't our event)\n    assert lock_instance._set_in_progress_code_slot == 2\n\n    await hass.config_entries.async_unload(lcm_entry.entry_id)"
}
```

> AGENT

Two issues:
1. The switch state is `unavailable` — the LCM setup is creating the switch entity but it depends on the coordinator which uses `get_usercodes` from ZWaveJSLock, and that's failing because there's no real Z-Wave connection.
2. The test hangs because the coordinator keeps retrying.

The full LCM setup needs the lock provider to work — `get_usercodes` needs to return something. I need to mock that on the ZWaveJSLock. Let me update `_setup_lcm_entry` to mock `async_get_usercodes`:

> TOOL
